- [ ] P2 tech seed (R2240 a): `src/os/queue_floor.py` - a read-only gauge that counts OPEN rows per queue against the self-drive standing floors (main >= 5, tech >= 10, explore >= 10) and reports the shortfall per queue in one line, so floor debt is measured instead of discovered mid-window. Criteria prefix AC-QF1..6 (contract, gated-row exclusion, floor table, honest zero, CLI codes, purity).
- [ ] P2 tech seed (R2240 b): `src/os/state_fold.py` - the state.json log fold has been executed by hand in several windows (archive-aware slicing, verbatim byte preservation, waterline gate); this module owns the fold predicate and the verbatim archive append behind one callable, with a --check mode. Criteria prefix AC-SF1..6 (waterline law, verbatim preservation, archive append, --check read-only, idempotence, purity).
- [ ] P2 tech seed (R2240 c): `state/debt.md` plus a checker - every window that ships a product face defers the reconcile battery, and the deferral currently lives only in prose; a machine-readable debt register (round, what is owed, since when, first-item flag) with a checker would stop the carried item from being re-derived by reading log prose. Criteria prefix AC-DEBT1..6 (register format, first-item flag, stale-debt alarm, checker CLI, honest empty, purity).
- [ ] P2 tech seed (R2240 d): status-export freshness gate - docs/status-export.json is required to carry a live line, a last artifact and a milestone within 48h; a checker that fails when the milestone window is stale or the three lines are missing would make the CEO-visible face self-policing. Criteria prefix AC-EXP1..6 (three-line contract, ISO8601 timestamps, 48h window check, stale verdict, CLI codes, purity).

- [x] P2 tech 队头认领（R2152·源=R2149 补种行·本行即册·判据先于代码）：**轮账本行可解析机检窗**——F3 律与 status-export 的 self_drive 派生都要求 state.json log 的 `R<n> tokens: local=… api=… api_reason=…` 行可被机械解析，而现无任何机检：行格式一旦漂移（缺 api=、reason 空、local 单位乱写、round 叙述行漏写账本行），派生面会静默变哑而不是变红。本窗交付一个解析器 + 审计面 + CLI 闸，并给坏形族与覆盖律正面覆盖。**AC-LGR1 正典解析**=`R2099 tokens: local=~120k api=0 api_reason=<text>` 解析出 round=2099 / local_raw="~120k" / local_value=120000 / local_approx=True / api=0 / api_approx=False / api_reason=text，且整数形 `local=95000`（陌生单位缺省）与小数单位形 `local=~1.2m`→1200000 解析一致。**AC-LGR2 坏形族 fail-closed**=八式逐一点名且零容忍：非 R 前缀 / 缺 local= / local 非数值或陌生单位 / 缺 api= / api 非纯整数（带单位或负号）/ reason 空或纯空白 / reason 短于 10 字符 / reason 含换行；**门序钉死**=round→local→api→reason（同时违反多门时只报最早一门，用三组交叠坏形实测）。**AC-LGR3 覆盖律**=round 叙述行（`^R<n>` 起首）与账本行按轮号互检：有叙述无账本→`rounds_without_ledger`，有账本无叙述→`ledger_without_round`，两者皆派生自实际行集（夹具 R1..R3 叙述 + 仅 R1/R3 账本 → missing=[2]，外加 R9 孤立账本 → orphan=[9]，零硬编码）。**AC-LGR4 尾窗语义**=审计支持 `tail=N` 只扫末 N 行并回声 账本行（`R<n> token:`）保持不判红但由 AC-LGR3 覆盖律兜住。**AC-LGR6 api 纪律**=api 恒为纯整数（`api=1k` 判红）、api>0 与 api=0 一律要求 reason 齐备且单行（无「无理由的空转」通道）。**AC-LGR7 CLI 契约**=`--check [FILE] [--tail N] [--json]`：干净退出 0 / 脏退出 2 / 文件缺失退出 3 / 未知旗标退出 2 且零改动；`--json` 输出 `json.loads` 可解且键集稳定、二次运行逐字节同一。**AC-LGR8 活闸与卫生**=对真实仓库 src/os/state.json log 实跑审计零坏形零覆盖缺（当前 30 叙述行 / 30 账本行实测基线）；模块源纯 ASCII、零网络 import、接线 reconcile_all 标签 - [x] P2 tech 队头认领（R2153·源=R2149 补种）：**套件双跑同一性口径窗**——R2143 已披露：含壁钟戳的种子世界每跑各自盖戳，故「双跑逐字节同一」只对判据结论成立、对整文字节不成立；现役套件对此口径不一（有的断言 sha16 同一、有的断言逐条结论同一）。扩展=统一口径并把两型判定写成可复用助手+一条跨套件自检。判据 **AC-RID1..AC-RID8 预注册于本行且先于代码**（七锚 `AC-RID`／`src/os/run_identity.py`／`test_run_identity.py`／`os-run-identity`／`run_identity`／`evidence_pairs`／`compare_runs` 对 HEAD 全仓 `git grep -F` 零占用 rc=1 实测 2026-10-27；诚实注：种子行里的占位 `AC-DRR` 已被本行自身占用，故正式前缀改用 AC-RID；`test_determinism` 在 `docs/copyright/.../source-program.txt` 与旧日志里是函数名 `test_determinism_two_runs` 的命中而非文件锚，为免歧义套件名改为 `test_run_identity.py`）：
  **AC-RID1 契约与两型互斥**=`normalize(text)` 只做三件被披露的规范化〔去 BOM／CRLF 与裸 CR 统一为 LF／末尾恰一个换行〕、不做任何时间戳/路径/摘要擦洗；`digest16` == 规范化 UTF-8 字节的 sha256 十六进制前 16 位小写；两型常量 `bytes`／`conclusions`；未知型 `E_BAD_MODE`；**两型互斥**：bytes 同一 ⇒ conclusions 同一恒成立，反向不成立，且比较器绝不把 conclusions 同一静默升级成 bytes 同一；
  **AC-RID2 判据行解析**=`PASS|FAIL <ID>` 行（ID=大写字母/数字/连字符且必含数字，`:` 或空格后为明细）解析为有序判据表；`SUITE PASS|FAIL n/m` 恰一行，零行 `E_NO_SUITE`、两行 `E_MULTI_SUITE`；同一 ID 出现两次 `E_DUP_ID`；其余行（表头、空行、折叠行、表格行）一律忽略且不误判；
  **AC-RID3 结论同一判定**=ID 集与逐条判决全等 ⇒ ok=True **即使明细载荷逐字不同**（R2143 披露的壁钟戳场景）；判决翻转／增条／减条 ⇒ ok=False 且 `differing` 按确定性顺序点名差异 ID；
  **AC-RID4 字节同一判定**=规范化后逐字节相等 ⇒ ok=True 且两摘要相等；任一处载荷差异（一个墙钟数字、一个临时路径）⇒ ok=False 且两摘要均回报；「结论同一而字节不同」的对照必须同时给出 bytes=False / conclusions=True；
  **AC-RID5 宣称检与假宣称律**=`byte-identical`／`bytes identical` ⇒ bytes 宣称；`criterion ... identical`／`conclusions identical`／`判据行...同一`／`判据结论同一` ⇒ conclusions 宣称；无宣称 ⇒ `E_NO_CLAIM`；`sha16 <HEX16>` 大小写不敏感解析；**宣称必须被验证**——文中宣称 byte-identical 而实测字节不同 ⇒ `E_FALSE_BYTE_CLAIM`；文中声明的 sha16 与另一跑 digest16 不等 ⇒ `E_FALSE_DIGEST`；
  **AC-RID6 目录配对**=按基名配对后缀 `-a/-b`、`-verifya/-verifyb`、`-run2`、`-2`；落单件与非 `.log` 件忽略、绝不猜配；记录按基名确定性排序；
  **AC-RID7 CLI 契约**=`--digest FILE`／`--pair A B [--mode]`／`--audit DIR`／`--json`；exit 0 干净／2 脏或坏参／3 文件缺失；JSON 键集稳定；**零写盘**（纯读工具）；
  **AC-RID8 活体自检与卫生**=对真实 `qa/` 目录审计得「结论漂移 0 条 + 假摘要 0 条」，假字节宣称计数并逐条打印（历史既存件入 `state/run_identity_baseline.json` 基线，只有新增转红）；产品源纯 ASCII/LF、零网络 import、零第三方依赖；接线 `reconcile_all` 在册 `os-run-identity(8)`。

首写胜出（资金环）**=flow `f1 grant 10` 于 D1 记账后，D2 以同 id 提交 `spend 40`：`flows_new=0`，落库行仍 (D1, grant, 10)，`account_ledger()` 终身仍 granted=10/spent=0/outstanding=10（资金账不因重提交而翻转），并登记一条 ring=flows 的冲突。**AC-FSW4 边界诚实**=登记面只覆盖两个资金环（orders/flows）；leads / dispatches 两环仍严格首写胜出（`leads_new=0`、原文不被改写、dispatch 路由不被重路由）但**不**登记分歧（导语文本分歧属 UGC 内容安全面，不由本台账裁决），且四环表行数在冲突批前后除新增首写外零变化。**AC-FSW5 读面契约**=`book_conflicts(day=None)` 信封恰三键 {conflict_count, by_ring, rows}，`conflict_count == len(rows) == sum(by_ring.values())`，行恰七键 {conflict_id, ring, natural_key, booked_day, booked_text, offered_day, offered_text}，行按 conflict_id 升序；空世界诚实 {0, {}, []}；坏 day 形 fail-closed 抛 E_FN_BAD_ARGS 且零读污染。**AC-FSW6 窗口律**=`book_conflicts(day=D2)` 只回首次提交日为 D2 的冲突（`offered_day` 是首次分歧提交日，重复提交不刷新它），无冲突日诚实空信封；`day=None` 读终身。**AC-FSW7 纯读与跨连接持久**=两次读面调用前后十表摘要与 `ledger_tx` 语义零变化（零写、零 token 移动）；两个独立连接（两个 FunnelDaily 实例指向同一 db）互相看得见对方登记的冲突；close/reopen 后登记面逐字节同一。**AC-FSW8 交付卫生**=源码纯 - [x] P2 tech 队头认领（R2157·源=R2152 收账补种行·判据 AC-LGB1..AC-LGB8 预注册于本行且先于代码）：**轮账本闸的基线语义窗**——R2152 的 CLI 已实现 `--baseline` / `--no-baseline`，但套件只覆盖了「有基线时活闸转绿」的一面，而基线本身是全闸唯一的豁免通道：谁能进基线、进了之后是否还被复核、坏基线怎么处置，全无断言。四种病态都真实：①豁免过宽（一条基线条目连坐压制多条缺口）；②基线陈旧（记了一个盘上早已补齐的轮号）时静默保留 → 基线变垃圾抽屉，日后真缺口的豁免真假难辨；③基线坏形（非 list / 非 int / 不可解析）在 `main` 的 except 面走成 `E_LGR_NO_STATE` 的错标签；④`--baseline` 指向不存在文件与 `--no-baseline` 是否同义无断言。判据 **AC-LGB1..AC-LGB8 预注册于本行且先于代码**（锚验 `git grep -F` 对 HEAD 逐锚实测：`stale_missing`／`stale_orphan`／`test_ledger_baseline.py`／`os-ledger-baseline` 四锚 rc=1 零占用；**诚实注两处**——`E_LGR_BAD_BASELINE` 在 HEAD 的 `ledger_lines.py` 已有 **2 处**（`load_baseline` 的两个 raise 面），本窗不是新建该码而是把它接进 CLI 的捕获面并加断言，故该锚非零占用；`--no-baseline` 是 R2152 既有旗标，本族只做语义等价断言）：
  **AC-LGB1 契约形（加法）**=`apply_baseline` 报告在 AC-LGR 既有键之外**追加** `stale_missing` / `stale_orphan` 两键（有序 int 列表），`baseline_missing` / `baseline_orphan` / `new_missing` / `new_orphan` / `clean` 语义逐值不变；`clean` 仍只由 errors + new_missing + new_orphan 决定。
  **AC-LGB2 豁免恰一件**=基线记 R7 而缺口为 {R7, R8} 时 `new_missing == [8]`、`clean is False`；记 {R7,R8} 时 `new_missing == []`、`clean is True`——一条基线条目只压制它自己那一个轮号，不连坐；`errors`（坏形行）永不被基线压制。
  **AC-LGB3 陈旧须见**=基线记了当前缺口集合里不存在的轮号（如缺口已补齐的 R5）时，该轮号出现在 `stale_missing`（或 `stale_orphan`）里且**不**出现在 `new_missing`；`stale` 本身不改变 `clean`（陈旧不是漂移，是维护信号）。
  **AC-LGB4 陈旧可见面**=文本报告在任一 stale 非空时恰多出一行 `stale: missing=<逗号列表|none> orphan=<逗号列表|none>`；两 stale 皆空时**不出现该行**（缺省面与 AC-LGR 逐字节同一）；`--json` 无论新旧都携 `stale_missing` / `stale_orphan` 两键。
  **AC-LGB5 坏基线 fail-closed**=三式坏形（顶层非 dict / `missing` 非 list / 元素非 int）与不可解析 JSON 一律 `E_LGR_BAD_BASELINE` 且 CLI 退 **3**、stdout 零输出、stderr 携该码（不再误标 `E_LGR_NO_STATE`）；坏基线文件字节前后逐字节同一、目录内零 `.tmp` 残留（工具永不改写基线）。
  **AC-LGB6 缺文件=空基线**=`--baseline <不存在路径>` 与 `--no-baseline` 的报告逐键同一（`baseline_missing == 0`、`stale_*` 为空、判决一致），且不创建该文件（零写）。

  **AC-EVDB1 契约与名族**=check_dir(path) 返回恰六键信封 {dir,file_count,tee_count,flagged_count,skipped_count,rows}，行恰六键 {name,class,bytes,crlf,bare_lf,utf8}；候选只取**目录直下、非符号链接、扩展名恰小写 `.log`** 的普通文件（子目录与非 .log 件一律不入候选且零递归），行序按 name 全序确定性排序。
  **AC-EVDB2 只读律**=check_dir 全程零写：候选件与目录条目的 bytes 与 st_mtime 调用前后逐件同一；缺目录/路径是文件一律抛 EvidenceDialectError（禁静默空集）。
  **AC-EVDB3 选择性修复**=fix_dir 只重写经 classify 判定 class != tee 的候选件；已健康件**零触碰**（bytes 与 mtime 逐件不变），非 .log 与非候选件同样零写。
  **AC-EVDB4 前后报读**=fix_dir 每行恰八键 {name,before_bytes,before_lines,after_bytes,after_lines,class_before,class_after,changed}；before/after 由**盘上实际字节**读回派生（before 取修复前原文、after 取修复后读回），行数按 `splitlines()` 计数；内容在换行轴外逐行不变（before 行表 == after 行表）。
  **AC-EVDB5 整批 fail-closed**=任一候选件不可修复（非 utf-8 等）时 fix_dir 抛 EvidenceDialectError 且**整批零写**（全部候选件 bytes 与 mtime 与调用前逐件同一），错误先行于任何落盘。
  **AC-EVDB6 回退界**=写入阶段中途失败（任一 write 抛错）时，已写成功的候选件全部**逐字节回退**到原始字节并原样抛错；不残留 `.evd-` 临时件。
  **AC-EVDB7 CLI 契约**=`--check-dir DIR` exit 0=全 tee / 2=有偏离 / 3=目录缺失，逐件打印 `check-dir name=<name> class=<class> bytes=<n> crlf=<n>` 且末行 `dir files=<n> tee=<n> flagged=<n>`；`--fix-dir DIR` exit 0 且逐件打印 `fix-dir name=<name> class=<before>-><after> bytes=<before>-><after> lines=<before>-><after>`、末行 `dir fixed=<n> skipped=<n>`；幂等=对已修复目录再跑 fix_dir 得 fixed=0 且零字节改动。
  **AC-EVDB8 卫生与接线**=模块与套件源纯 ASCII、真 import 行零网络库（R1678 自指假阳性教训：套件断言不得以自身源码文本为针）、无墙钟依赖；reconcile_all SUITES 在册 `os-evidence-writer-dir(8)`；既有单件面（--check/--fix/write_evidence 契约）逐字节保真零回归。
  〔**done 2026-10-28 R2172**〕交付=`src/os/evidence_writer.py` 增 `check_dir` / `fix_dir` / `_dir_entries` / `_raw_lines` / `_restore` 与 CLI `--check-dir` / `--fix-dir`（六键信封+六键 census 行；候选=目录直下非链接小写 `.log` 普通件且零递归；修复前**整批**完成分类与 utf-8 严格解码=任一不可修复件整批零写；写入阶段中途失败按原字节逐件回退且不残留 `.evd-` 临时件；每行 before/after 字节与行数一律由盘上字节读回派生，且断言行表在换行轴外不变）。套件 `src/os/test_evidence_writer_dir.py` SUITE PASS 8/8 **双跑 stdout 逐字节同一** sha16 `52d7cd6fcf9ff17f`（537B·qa/evidence-writer-dir-R2172-a.log / -b.log·单写者 utf-8 LF）；接线 `os-evidence-writer-dir(8)`；相邻既有套件 test_evidence_writer.py 8/8 全绿（零回归）。**实弹读数**=`--check-dir qa` 得 `files=1974 tee=1445 flagged=529`（历史 ps-utf16/other 方言件），批量改历史证据不在本轮授权面=**不整体改**；但 census `--check` 另报一件未入基线的漂移件 `qa/reconcile-all-R2171.log`（4477 行中 35 行 CRLF，正是本工具的目标缺陷类）→**本窗用产品修了这一件**：`--fix` 得 class=tee bytes=711887→711852 crlf=0 且行表 4477 行不变，随后 census 读数 **DRIFT-CLEAN files=1974 flags=0**。诚实注=①**孤 CR 不被 census 识别**（`b"three\rfour\r"` 分类仍为 tee，crlf 计数只数 `\r\n` 对）——批量面按选择性律不修它，属既有 census 探测力边界，已如实钉进 AC-EVDB4 夹具而非放宽实现；②AC-EVDB6 首跑红于断言用了 mtime（回退按原字节重写必然刷新 mtime），改判为**逐字节**同一，Criterion 原文即「逐字节回退」；③全量电池与矩阵再生本轮未跑（25 分钟预算），列下轮首项。
- [x] P2 tech head claim (R2225, the R2223/R2224 carried fold row; criteria AC-FLD1..AC-FLD8 registered here BEFORE the surgery): **state.json waterline fold under the archive-aware coverage law**. R2223 proved the fold and the old live-gate law were mutually unsatisfiable; R2224 made the coverage law scope-aware (counts from live+archive, drift live-only), which unblocks exactly this surgery. AC-FLD1 eol-clean pre-state: `logs_toolkit.py eol --fix --execute` reports post_bare_lf=0 written=True and `--fix-indent --execute` reports post_indent_ok=True before any fold. AC-FLD2 window law: exactly one of --window-tail/--window-slice, the folded slice is 1:200 and the newest 33 lines stay live. AC-FLD3 token law: the tool's own PAT catalog over the window is named in full in the one-line summary, so token_missing==[] and token_ok==true on the dry run BEFORE the execute. AC-FLD4 byte law: predicted_bytes+reserve_bytes <= 78500 and the post-write `gauge` reports fold_needed=False with margin_bytes>0. AC-FLD5 verbatim law: the archive section carries the window lines byte-verbatim and `census` reads section integrity with zero unknown mismatches. AC-FLD6 scoped-gate law: `ledger_lines.py --archives logs` reads live_lines plus archive_lines with ledger/round counts from the union and stays VERDICT CLEAN, while the two shrunken live windows no longer gate. AC-FLD7 regression law: os-ledger-lines, os-ledger-baseline and os-ledger-closeout all 8/8 green on the folded tree. AC-FLD8 verification law: the full reconcile battery plus the suite-matrix regeneration run on the committed folded tree (never a mixed tree), all suites green, matrix --check byte-identical, census DRIFT-CLEAN. - [x] P2 tech claim (R2233 seed, discharged 2026-10-29 R2238): **retry-naming convention as a gate rule**. Born from the R2233 red: the R2230 battery stored its post-fix rerun as `reconcile-all-R2230-run2.log`, and AC-RID6 reads the `-run2` suffix as the second leg of a double-run byte-identity pair, so 420 suite-internal 'byte-identical' sentences in the body  were read as a file-pair claim (E_FALSE_BYTE_CLAIM, disclosed into state/run_identity_baseline.json this window). Gap: a correction rerun and an identity pair are different things that share one suffix. Criteria prefix AC-RTN1..AC-RTN6 (contract; the writer/naming helper emits `-retry` for any rerun of an evidence stem that already  exists and whose RUNNER verdict differs, and refuses `-run2`/`-2`  for that case; the identity auditor never pairs a `-retry` leg; a fixture pair that genuinely claims byte identity still turns red; the live baseline stops growing by construction for this class, so the R2153/R2233 disclosures stay as history rather than precedent; purity and wiring into reconcile_all). [claimed+done 2026-10-29 R2238] Anchor check at HEAD: `retry_naming` / `_retry_stem` / `test_os_retry_naming` / `os-retry-naming` were `git grep -F` zero-occupancy rc=1 in the code face (the single AC-RTN hit was the R2233 closeout prose itself). Delivered: `src/os/run_identity.py` now owns the one retry predicate (`RETRY_SUFFIX="-retry"`, `RETRY_RE`, `is_retry_name`) and `find_pairs` skips every retry leg before it can become an identity leg, so a correction rerun's body can never be read as a file-pair byte claim (the R2233 E_FALSE_BYTE_CLAIM class). New `src/os/retry_naming.py`: `base_stem` / `is_retry` / `check_name` (refuses `-run2`/`-2` with E_RETRY_BAD_SUFFIX and a retry leg with E_RETRY_BAD_NAME) / `retry_name` (`-retry`, then `-retry2`, `-retry3`, base preserved) / `resolve_rerun` (first run keeps its stem, an existing stem becomes a retry and reports the RUNNER verdict move, an identity-pair leg is refused with E_RETRY_IDENTITY_SUFFIX) / `audit_dir` (read-only census classing every leg first|retry|legacy-rerun|identity-leg) plus the `--check-dir` / `--next` CLI (exit 0 clean, 2 legacy rerun names, 3 missing dir). Evidence: SUITE PASS 9/9 double-run byte-identical sha16 1d7cddf2cc895728 (qa/os-retry-naming-R2238-a.log / -b.log, 563 bytes each, class=tee crlf=0 utf8=True through src/os/evidence_R2238.py, never a shell redirection), wired as `os-retry-naming`(9) in reconcile_all; adjacent os-run-identity / os-evidence-writer-dir / os-ledger-lines all 8/8 green and the live identity audit still gated clean with the new pair present. Honest notes: (1) green 9/9 only after three this-window test-side fixes (a leg the fixture never wrote, the `_counterpart` left-leg branch that matched only when a pair's right suffix was empty, and a census count that forgot the q-retry leg) - no criterion was widened; (2) the shipped baseline is unchanged and needs no entry: no `-retry` file exists in qa/ yet, so the rule is forward-looking by construction; (3) the full reconcile battery and the suite-matrix regeneration were NOT run in this 25-minute window and are the R2239 first item.
